     1	import { DefaultTheme, ThemeProvider } from "@react-navigation/native";
     2	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";
     3	import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
     4	import * as NavigationBar from "expo-navigation-bar";
     5	import {
     6	  Animated,
     7	  AppState,
     8	  Platform,
     9	  Text,
    10	  TextInput,
    11	  View,
    12	} from "react-native";
    13	import {
    14	  SafeAreaProvider,
    15	  useSafeAreaInsets,
    16	} from "react-native-safe-area-context";
    17	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
    18	
    19	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
    20	import MainScreenPushFrame from "../components/MainScreenPushFrame";
    21	import ScreenFade from "../components/ScreenFade";
    22	import StickyCartButton from "../components/StickyCartButton";
    23	import {
    24	  BackgroundHeroStateProvider,
    25	  useBackgroundHeroState,
    26	} from "../utils/backgroundHeroStateContext";
    27	import { HeaderScrollProvider } from "../utils/headerScrollContext";
    28	import {
    29	  HeaderSwipeProvider,
    30	  useHeaderSwipe,
    31	} from "../utils/headerSwipeContext";
    32	import { getTopSafeInset } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    33	import { ShopCartProvider, useShopCart } from "../utils/shopCartContext";
    34	
    35	const navigationTheme = {
    36	  ...DefaultTheme,
    37	  colors: {
    38	    ...DefaultTheme.colors,
    39	    background:
    40	      Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.background,
    41	    card: Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.card,
    42	  },
    43	};
    44	const androidNavigationBarColor = "#f7b967";
    45	const androidNavigationBarHairlineColor = "rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.28)";
    46	const androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth = 0.375;
    47	const androidNavigationBarButtonStyle = "light";
    48	
    49	function disableAutomaticFontScaling(Component) {
    50	  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
    51	  Component.defaultProps.allowFontScaling = false;
    52	  Component.defaultProps.maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1;
    53	}
    54	
    55	disableAutomaticFontScaling(Text);
    56	disableAutomaticFontScaling(TextInput);
    57	
    58	async function applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme() {
    59	  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
    60	
    61	  try {
    62	    await NavigationBar.setVisibilityAsync("visible");
    63	  } catch {}
    64	
    65	  try {
    66	    await NavigationBar.setButtonStyleAsync(androidNavigationBarButtonStyle);
    67	  } catch {}
    68	}
    69	
    70	function AndroidNavigationBarTint({ pathname }) {
    71	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    72	
    73	  useEffect(() => {
    74	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") return undefined;
    75	
    76	    applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    77	    const restoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 250);
    78	    const finalRestoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 1000);
    79	    const appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
    80	      if (state === "active") {
    81	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    82	      }
    83	    });
    84	    let visibilitySubscription;
    85	
    86	    try {
    87	      visibilitySubscription = NavigationBar.addVisibilityListener(
    88	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme
    89	      );
    90	    } catch {}
    91	
    92	    return () => {
    93	      clearTimeout(restoreTimer);
    94	      clearTimeout(finalRestoreTimer);
    95	      appStateSubscription.remove();
    96	      visibilitySubscription?.remove();
    97	    };
    98	  }, [pathname]);
    99	
   100	  if (Platform.OS !== "android" || insets.bottom <= 0) {
   101	    return null;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  return (
   105	    <View
   106	      pointerEvents="none"
   107	      style={{
   108	        position: "absolute",
   109	        left: 0,
   110	        right: 0,
   111	        bottom: 0,
   112	        height: insets.bottom,
   113	        borderTopWidth: androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth,
   114	        borderTopColor: androidNavigationBarHairlineColor,
   115	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   116	        zIndex: 1000001,
   117	        elevation: 1000001,
   118	      }}
   119	    />
   120	  );
   121	}
   122	
   123	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
   124	  const pathname = usePathname();
   125	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   126	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   127	  const { cartOverlayActionRequest, isShopOverlayVisible } = useShopCart();
   128	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   129	  const topSafeInset = getTopSafeInset(insets);
   130	
   131	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
   132	
   133	  const useOverlayHeader =
   134	    pathname === "/" ||
   135	    pathname === "/products" ||
   136	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
   137	    pathname === "/contact";
   138	
   139	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
   140	    useOverlayHeader &&
   141	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
   142	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
   143	  const screenFadeTopOffset = pathname === "/shop"
   144	    ? 120 + topSafeInset
   145	    : 84 + topSafeInset;
   146	  const shouldShowScreenFade =
   147	    !(pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible);
   148	  const shouldShowCartOpeningDim =
   149	    cartOverlayActionRequest.pending && pathname !== "/shop";
   150	
   151	  return (
   152	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
   153	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   154	        <View
   155	          pointerEvents="none"
   156	          style={{
   157	            position: "absolute",
   158	            top: 0,
   159	            left: 0,
   160	            right: 0,
   161	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   162	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   163	          }}
   164	        >
   165	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
   166	        </View>
   167	      ) : null}
   168	
   169	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
   170	        <Stack
   171	          screenOptions={{
   172	            headerShown: false,
   173	            animation: "none",
   174	            contentStyle: {
   175	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
   176	                ? "transparent"
   177	                : "#FFFCF2",
   178	            },
   179	          }}
   180	        />
   181	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
   182	
   183	      {shouldShowScreenFade ? (
   184	        <ScreenFade
   185	          showTopFade={!useOverlayHeader}
   186	          topOffset={screenFadeTopOffset}
   187	        />
   188	      ) : null}
   189	
   190	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   191	        <View
   192	          pointerEvents="box-none"
   193	          style={{
   194	            position: "absolute",
   195	            top: 0,
   196	            left: 0,
   197	            right: 0,
   198	            zIndex: 1000000,
   199	            elevation: 1000000,
   200	          }}
   201	        >
   202	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
   203	        </View>
   204	      ) : null}
   205	
   206	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
   207	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   208	      ) : null}
   209	
   210	      {shouldShowCartOpeningDim ? (
   211	        <View
   212	          pointerEvents="none"
   213	          style={{
   214	            position: "absolute",
   215	            top: 0,
   216	            right: 0,
   217	            bottom: 0,
   218	            left: 0,
   219	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   220	            zIndex: 1000001,
   221	            elevation: 1000001,
   222	          }}
   223	        />
   224	      ) : null}
   225	
   226	      <StickyCartButton />
   227	
   228	      <AndroidNavigationBarTint pathname={pathname} />
   229	    </View>
   230	  );
   231	}
   232	
   233	export default function RootLayout() {
   234	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   235	
   236	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   237	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   238	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   239	    "TT Fors Demibold": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_demibold.ttf"),
   240	    "TT Fors Light": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_light.ttf"),
   241	  });
   242	
   243	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   244	    return null;
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  return (
   248	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   249	      <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   250	        <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   251	          <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   252	            <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   253	              <ShopCartProvider>
   254	                <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   255	              </ShopCartProvider>
   256	            </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   257	          </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   258	        </HeaderScrollProvider>
   259	      </ThemeProvider>
   260	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   261	  );
   262	}

FILE: mob/app/index.js

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import {
     3	  Animated,
     4	  Image,
     5	  StyleSheet,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	  useWindowDimensions,
     9	} from "react-native";
    10	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
    11	
    12	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
    13	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
    14	import PageDivider from "../components/PageDivider";
    15	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
    16	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    17	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    18	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    19	
    20	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    21	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    22	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    23	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    24	const homeFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    25	const homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    26	
    27	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    28	  return (
    29	    currentLayout &&
    30	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    31	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    32	  );
    33	}
    34	
    35	export default function HomeScreen() {
    36	  const [imageLayouts, setImageLayouts] = useState({});
    37	  const [paragraphLayouts, setParagraphLayouts] = useState({});
    38	  const {
    39	    compactTopLayout,
    40	    initialContentOffset,
    41	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    42	    scrollHandlers,
    43	    scrollY,
    44	  } =
    45	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    46	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    47	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    48	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
    49	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    50	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    51	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    52	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageHeight],
    53	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    54	  });
    55	  const handleParagraphLayout = useCallback(
    56	    (featureName) =>
    57	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    58	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    59	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    60	
    61	        setParagraphLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    62	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    63	            return currentLayouts;
    64	          }
    65	
    66	          return {
    67	            ...currentLayouts,
    68	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    69	          };
    70	        });
    71	      },
    72	    []
    73	  );
    74	  const handleImageLayout = useCallback(
    75	    (featureName) =>
    76	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    77	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    78	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    79	
    80	        setImageLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    81	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    82	            return currentLayouts;
    83	          }
    84	
    85	          return {
    86	            ...currentLayouts,
    87	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    88	          };
    89	        });
    90	      },
    91	    []
    92	  );
    93	
    94	  return (
    95	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    96	      <Animated.ScrollView
    97	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
    98	        contentContainerStyle={[
    99	          sharedStyles.scrollContent,
   100	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   101	        ]}
   102	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   103	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   104	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   105	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   106	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   107	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   108	        {...scrollHandlers}
   109	      >
   110	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
   111	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   112	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   113	            pageTitleStyle={[sharedStyles.pageTitle, homeStyles.pageTitle]}
   114	            scrollY={scrollY}
   115	          />
   116	
   117	          <CenterMagnifyView
   118	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.passion}
   119	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.passion}
   120	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   121	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   122	            scrollY={scrollY}
   123	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   124	          >
   125	            <View
   126	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("passion")}
   127	              style={[
   128	                sharedStyles.directionalImageBlendWrap,
   129	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
   130	              ]}
   131	            >
   132	              <Image
   133	                pointerEvents="none"
   134	                source={require("../passion111_mos9_bright_italian_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   135	                style={[
   136	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   137	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   138	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   139	                ]}
   140	                resizeMode="contain"
   141	              />
   142	              <Animated.View
   143	                pointerEvents="none"
   144	                style={[
   145	                  sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendPanel,
   146	                  {
   147	                    height:
   148	                      croppedImageHeight + passionImageBlendFeatherHeight,
   149	                    transform: [{ translateY: passionImageBlendTranslateY }],
   150	                  },
   151	                ]}
   152	              >
   153	                <LinearGradient
   154	                  colors={[
   155	                    "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
   156	                    passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   157	                  ]}
   158	                  locations={[0, 1]}
   159	                  style={sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendFeather}
   160	                />
   161	                <View
   162	                  style={[
   163	                    sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendSolid,
   164	                    {
   165	                      height: croppedImageHeight,
   166	                      backgroundColor: passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   167	                    },
   168	                  ]}
   169	                />
   170	              </Animated.View>
   171	            </View>
   172	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   173	              Passion
   174	            </Text>
   175	            <Text
   176	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("passion")}
   177	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   178	            >
   179	              Every Alla Vostra board is prepared with care, precision, and a
   180	              love for the grazing experience.
   181	            </Text>
   182	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   183	
   184	          <PageDivider />
   185	
   186	          <CenterMagnifyView
   187	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.taste}
   188	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.taste}
   189	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   190	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   191	            scrollY={scrollY}
   192	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   193	          >
   194	            <View
   195	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("taste")}
   196	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   197	            >
   198	              <Image
   199	                pointerEvents="none"
   200	                source={require("../taste111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   201	                style={[
   202	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   203	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   204	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   205	                ]}
   206	                resizeMode="contain"
   207	              />
   208	            </View>
   209	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   210	              Taste
   211	            </Text>
   212	            <Text
   213	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("taste")}
   214	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   215	            >
   216	              Our boards are built around layered flavor: cheeses, meats,
   217	              fruits, sweets, spreads, and accoutrements.
   218	            </Text>
   219	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   220	
   221	          <PageDivider />
   222	
   223	          <CenterMagnifyView
   224	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.convenience}
   225	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.convenience}
   226	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   227	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   228	            scrollY={scrollY}
   229	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   230	          >
   231	            <View
   232	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("convenience")}
   233	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   234	            >
   235	              <Image
   236	                pointerEvents="none"
   237	                source={require("../convenience111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   238	                style={[
   239	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   240	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   241	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   242	                ]}
   243	                resizeMode="contain"
   244	              />
   245	            </View>
   246	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   247	              Convenience
   248	            </Text>
   249	            <Text
   250	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("convenience")}
   251	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   252	            >
   253	              Alla Vostra brings the board to you for celebrations, family
   254	              gatherings, and larger events.
   255	            </Text>
   256	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   257	        </View>
   258	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   259	    </View>
   260	  );
   261	}
   262	
   263	const homeStyles = StyleSheet.create({
   264	  pageTitle: {
   265	    lineHeight: 64.5,
   266	  },
   267	
   268	  featureTitle: {
   269	    lineHeight: 57,
   270	  },
   271	
   272	  featureText: {
   273	    lineHeight: 65.25,
   274	  },
   275	});

    93	function ProductSection({ product, croppedImageWidth }) {
    94	  return (
    95	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
    96	      <View style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>
    97	        <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
    98	          <Image
    99	            pointerEvents="none"
   100	            source={product.image}
   101	            style={[
   102	              productsStyles.productImage,
   103	              { height: croppedImageWidth },
   104	            ]}
   105	            resizeMode="contain"
   106	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   107	          />
   108	        </View>
   109	      </View>
   110	
   111	      <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   112	      <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   113	
   114	      <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   115	        {product.includes.map((section) => (
   116	          <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   117	            <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   118	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   119	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   120	            </View>
   121	
   122	            {section.items.map((item) => (
   123	              <View key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItemRow}>
   124	                <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleBorder}>
   125	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleOuter} />
   126	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangle} />
   127	                </View>
   128	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeItem}>{item}</Text>
   129	              </View>
   130	            ))}
   131	
   132	            {section.note ? (
   133	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   134	            ) : null}
   135	          </View>
   136	        ))}
   137	      </View>
   138	    </View>
   139	  );
   140	}
   141	
   142	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   143	  const {
   144	    compactTopLayout,
   145	    initialContentOffset,
   146	    scrollContentInsetStyle,

   148	    scrollY,
   149	  } =

   151	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
   152	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   153	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
   154	
   155	  return (
   156	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   157	      <Animated.ScrollView
   158	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   159	        contentContainerStyle={[
   160	          productsStyles.scrollContent,
   161	          scrollContentInsetStyle,

   175	            scrollY={scrollY}
   176	          />
   177	
   178	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   179	            <Fragment key={product.title}>
   180	              <CenterMagnifyView

   183	              >
   184	                <ProductSection
   185	                  product={product}

   187	                />
   188	              </CenterMagnifyView>
   189	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   190	            </Fragment>
   191	          ))}
   192	        </View>
   193	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   194	    </View>
   195	  );

FILE: mob/app/contact.js

     1	import { Animated, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     4	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     5	import contactStyles from "../styles/contactStyles";
     6	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
     7	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
     8	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
     9	
    10	export default function ContactScreen() {
    11	  const {
    12	    compactTopLayout,
    13	    initialContentOffset,
    14	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    15	    scrollHandlers,
    16	    scrollY,
    17	  } =
    18	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    19	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    20	
    21	  return (
    22	    <View style={contactStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    23	      <Animated.ScrollView
    24	        style={contactStyles.scroll}
    25	        contentContainerStyle={[
    26	          contactStyles.scrollContent,
    27	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
    28	        ]}
    29	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    30	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    31	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    32	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    33	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
    34	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
    35	        {...scrollHandlers}
    36	      >
    37	        <View style={contactStyles.main}>
    38	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    39	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    40	            pageTitleStyle={contactStyles.pageTitle}
    41	            scrollY={scrollY}

    63	            </CenterMagnifyView>

    88	              />
    89	            </CenterMagnifyView>
    90	
    91	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
    92	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Your Message</Text>
    93	              <TextInput
    94	                style={[contactStyles.input, contactStyles.messageInput]}
    95	                placeholder="Your Message"
    96	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
    97	                multiline
    98	                returnKeyType="default"
    99	                textAlignVertical="top"
   100	              />
   101	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   102	
   103	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY} style={contactStyles.buttonWrap}>
   104	              <View style={contactStyles.button}>
   105	                <Text style={contactStyles.buttonText}>Send Message</Text>
   106	              </View>
   107	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   108	          </View>
   109	        </View>
   110	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   111	    </View>
   112	  );
   113	}

   424	function PiccolaQuantityTriangle({ direction, muted }) {
   425	  const strokeInset = piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth / 2;
   426	  const fillColor = muted ? "#FBDCB3" : "#f7b967";
   427	  const strokeColor = muted ? "#888888" : "#111111";
   428	  const path =
   429	    direction === "up"
   430	      ? [
   431	          `M ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${strokeInset}`,
   432	          `L ${
   433	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   434	          } ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset}`,
   435	          `L ${strokeInset} ${
   436	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   437	          }`,
   438	          "Z",
   439	        ].join(" ")
   440	      : [
   441	          `M ${strokeInset} ${strokeInset}`,
   442	          `L ${
   443	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   444	          } ${strokeInset}`,
   445	          `L ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${
   446	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   447	          }`,
   448	          "Z",
   449	        ].join(" ");
   450	
   451	  return (
   452	    <Svg
   453	      height="100%"
   454	      style={shopStyles.piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleSvg}
   455	      viewBox={`0 0 ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth} ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}`}
   456	      width="100%"
   457	    >
   458	      <Path
   459	        d={path}
   460	        fill={fillColor}
   461	        stroke={strokeColor}
   462	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   463	        strokeWidth={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth}
   464	      />
   465	    </Svg>
   466	  );
   467	}

   530	export default function ShopScreen() {
   531	  const { openCart } = useLocalSearchParams();
   532	  const initialOpenCartRequest = Array.isArray(openCart)
   533	    ? openCart[0]

   926	  const overlayVisibleArrowOpacity = overlayHeldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   927	    inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],

   985	  const getOverlayProductTransitionDirection = (fromName, toName) => {
   986	    const fromIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   987	      (product) => product.name === fromName,
   988	    );
   989	    const toIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   990	      (product) => product.name === toName,
   991	    );
   992	
   993	    if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) {
   994	      return -1;
   995	    }
   996	
   997	    const lastOverlayIndex = overlayNavProducts.length - 1;
   998	
   999	    if (fromIndex === lastOverlayIndex && toIndex === 0) return -1;
  1000	    if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastOverlayIndex) return 1;
  1001	
  1002	    return toIndex > fromIndex ? -1 : 1;
  1003	  };
  1004	
  1005	  const transitionOverlayProductImage = (nextProductName, direction) => {
  1006	    if (!nextProductName || nextProductName === activeOverlayProductName) {
  1007	      return;
  1008	    }
  1009	
  1010	    if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  1011	      const previousAnimation = overlayImageAnimationRef.current;
  1012	      overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  1013	      previousAnimation.stop();
  1014	    }
  1015	
  1016	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  1017	      const previousNavAnimation = overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current;
  1018	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  1019	      previousNavAnimation.stop();
  1020	    }
  1021	
  1022	    const nextOverlayNavIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  1023	      (product) => product.name === nextProductName,
  1024	    );
  1025	
  1026	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  1027	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(activeOverlayProductName);
  1028	    setOverlayImageDirection(direction);
  1029	    overlayImageProgress.setValue(0);
  1030	    setActiveOverlayProductName(nextProductName);
  1031	
  1032	    if (nextOverlayNavIndex >= 0) {
  1033	      const navAnimation = Animated.timing(overlayNavIndicatorProgress, {
  1034	        toValue: nextOverlayNavIndex,
  1035	        duration: 320,
  1036	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  1037	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1038	      });
  1039	
  1040	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = navAnimation;
  1041	      navAnimation.start(() => {
  1042	        if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current === navAnimation) {
  1043	          overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  1044	        }
  1045	      });
  1046	    }
  1047	
  1048	    const animation = Animated.timing(overlayImageProgress, {
  1049	      toValue: 1,
  1050	      duration: 320,
  1051	      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  1052	      useNativeDriver: true,
  1053	    });
  1054	
  1055	    overlayImageAnimationRef.current = animation;
  1056	
  1057	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
  1058	      if (finished) {
  1059	        setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);
  1060	      }
  1061	
  1062	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current === animation) {
  1063	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  1064	      }
  1065	    });
  1066	  };
  1067	
  1068	  const handleOverlayProductNameSelect = (nextProductName) => {
  1069	    const direction = getOverlayProductTransitionDirection(
  1070	      activeOverlayProductName,
  1071	      nextProductName,
  1072	    );
  1073	
  1074	    transitionOverlayProductImage(nextProductName, direction);
  1075	  };

  1077	  const renderOverlayArrowChevron = (direction, muted = false) => (
  1078	    <View
  1079	      style={[
  1080	        shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevron,
  1081	        muted && shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronMuted,
  1082	        direction === "left"
  1083	          ? shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronLeft
  1084	          : shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronRight,
  1085	      ]}
  1086	    />
  1087	  );

  1089	  const goToOverlayPreviousProduct = () => {
  1090	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  1091	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName,
  1092	    );
  1093	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
  1094	
  1095	    const previousIndex =
  1096	      (currentIndex + overlayNavProducts.length - 1) %
  1097	      overlayNavProducts.length;
  1098	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
  1099	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[previousIndex].name, 1);
  1100	  };
  1101	
  1102	  const goToOverlayNextProduct = () => {
  1103	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  1104	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName,
  1105	    );
  1106	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
  1107	
  1108	    const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % overlayNavProducts.length;
  1109	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
  1110	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[nextIndex].name, -1);
  1111	  };
  1112	
  1113	  const getOverlayBandTouchPoint = (event) => {
  1114	    const primaryTouch =
  1115	      event?.nativeEvent?.touches?.[0] ||
  1116	      event?.nativeEvent?.changedTouches?.[0] ||
  1117	      event?.nativeEvent;
  1118	
  1119	    return {
  1120	      x:
  1121	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageX === "number"
  1122	          ? primaryTouch.pageX
  1123	          : primaryTouch?.locationX,
  1124	      y:
  1125	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageY === "number"
  1126	          ? primaryTouch.pageY
  1127	          : primaryTouch?.locationY,
  1128	    };
  1129	  };
  1130	
  1131	  const handleOverlayBandTouchStart = (event) => {
  1132	    const { x, y } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  1133	
  1134	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = x;
  1135	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = y;
  1136	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  1137	    showOverlayHeldArrows();
  1138	  };
  1139	
  1140	  const handleOverlayBandTouchMove = (event) => {
  1141	    if (overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current) return;
  1142	
  1143	    const startX = overlaySwipeStartXRef.current;
  1144	    const startY = overlaySwipeStartYRef.current;
  1145	    const { x: currentX, y: currentY } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  1146	
  1147	    if (
  1148	      typeof startX !== "number" ||
  1149	      typeof startY !== "number" ||
  1150	      typeof currentX !== "number" ||
  1151	      typeof currentY !== "number"
  1152	    ) {
  1153	      return;
  1154	    }
  1155	
  1156	    const dx = currentX - startX;
  1157	    const dy = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
  1158	    const minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance = 12.5;
  1159	    const horizontalDominanceRatio = 0.4375;
  1160	    const isHorizontalSwipeIntent =
  1161	      Math.abs(dx) >= minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance &&
  1162	      Math.abs(dx) > dy * horizontalDominanceRatio;
  1163	
  1164	    if (!isHorizontalSwipeIntent) return;
  1165	
  1166	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = true;
  1167	
  1168	    if (dx < 0) {
  1169	      goToOverlayNextProduct();
  1170	    } else {
  1171	      goToOverlayPreviousProduct();
  1172	    }
  1173	  };
  1174	
  1175	  const handleOverlayBandTouchEnd = () => {
  1176	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = null;
  1177	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = null;
  1178	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  1179	    hideOverlayHeldArrows();
  1180	  };
  1181	
  1182	  useEffect(() => {
  1183	    if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
  1184	      clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
  1185	    }
  1186	
  1187	    overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
  1188	      clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger();
  1189	      overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = null;
  1190	    }, 130);
  1191	
  1192	    return () => {
  1193	      if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
  1194	        clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
  1195	      }
  1196	    };
  1197	  }, [activeOverlayProductName]);
  1198	
  1199	  useEffect(() => {
  1200	    return () => {
  1201	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  1202	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current.stop();
  1203	      }
  1204	
  1205	      if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  1206	        overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current.stop();

  1208	    };
  1209	  }, []);

  1644	  );

  1655	            (cartOverlayScrollY / cartOverlayCreamScrollbarScrollRange) *
  1656	              cartOverlayCreamScrollbarTravel,
  1657	          ),
  1658	        )
  1659	      : 0;

  1661	    overlayCartBillableProducts.length > 0 ? cartOverlayDeliveryFee : 0;

  1690	      cartOverlayGrandTotalTargetWidth + 0.5;

  1698	      }

  1701	    });

  4224	                      numberOfLines={1}

    77	const pageLabels = {

    93	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    94	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    95	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    96	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    97	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    98	  return "home";
    99	}
   100	
   101	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
   102	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
   103	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
   104	
   105	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
   106	
   107	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   108	
   109	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
   110	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
   111	
   112	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
   113	}

   115	export default function AppHeader({
   116	  activePage,
   117	  dimHeaderExceptShopButton = false,
   118	  scrollY,
   119	  showCarousel = true,
   120	  showHero = true,
   121	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
   122	  showOnlyHero = false,
   123	}) {
   124	  const pathname = usePathname();
   125	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   126	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   127	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
   128	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   129	  const activePageIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
   130	  const handlesCarouselVisuals = showCarousel && !showOnlyHero;
   131	
   132	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   133	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
   134	  const canNavigateWithHeader = useHeaderNavigationGate(safeScrollY);
   135	  const heroStateScrollY =
   136	    showHero && backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen && backgroundHeroState?.heroScrollY
   137	      ? backgroundHeroState.heroScrollY
   138	      : safeScrollY;
   139	  const headerMotionScrollY = showOnlyHero ? heroStateScrollY : safeScrollY;
   140	
   141	  const fallbackHeldArrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   142	  const fallbackRouteTransitionProgress = useRef(
   143	    new Animated.Value(0)
   144	  ).current;
   145	  const heldArrowOpacity =
   146	    screenSwipe?.heldArrowOpacity || fallbackHeldArrowOpacity;
   147	  const routeTransitionProgress =
   148	    screenSwipe?.routeTransitionProgress || fallbackRouteTransitionProgress;
   149	  const indicatorProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(activePageIndex)).current;
   150	  const fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity = useRef(
   151	    new Animated.Value(0)
   152	  ).current;
   153	  const fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity = useRef(
   154	    new Animated.Value(0)
   155	  ).current;
   156	  const fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression = useRef(
   157	    new Animated.Value(0)
   158	  ).current;
   159	  const directionalLeftArrowOpacity =
   160	    screenSwipe?.directionalLeftArrowOpacity ||
   161	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity;
   162	  const directionalRightArrowOpacity =
   163	    screenSwipe?.directionalRightArrowOpacity ||
   164	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity;
   165	  const directionalArrowBaseSuppression =
   166	    screenSwipe?.directionalArrowBaseSuppression ||
   167	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression;
   168	
   169	  const indicatorAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   170	  const indicatorIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   171	  const suppressCarouselPressUntilRef = useRef(0);
   172	  const canNavigateWithHeaderRef = useRef(canNavigateWithHeader);
   173	
   174	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   175	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   176	  canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current = canNavigateWithHeader;
   177	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   178	  activeIndexRef.current = activePageIndex;
   179	
   180	  useEffect(() => {
   181	    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
   182	
   183	    const resolvedCheeseboardImage = Image.resolveAssetSource(
   184	      cheeseboardProductsImage
   185	    );
   186	
   187	    if (resolvedCheeseboardImage?.uri) {
   188	      Image.prefetch(resolvedCheeseboardImage.uri).catch(() => {});
   189	    }
   190	  }, []);
   191	
   192	  const showHeldArrows = (event = null) => {
   193	    if (screenSwipe?.showHeldArrowHint) {
   194	      screenSwipe.showHeldArrowHint(event);
   195	      return;
   196	    }
   197	
   198	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   199	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   200	  };
   201	
   202	  const hideHeldArrows = () => {
   203	    if (screenSwipe?.hideHeldArrowHint) {
   204	      screenSwipe.hideHeldArrowHint();
   205	      return;
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   209	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   210	  };
   211	
   212	  const cancelHeldArrows = () => {
   213	    if (screenSwipe?.cancelHeldArrowHint) {
   214	      screenSwipe.cancelHeldArrowHint();
   215	      return;
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    hideHeldArrows();
   219	  };
   220	
   221	  const updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove = (event) => {
   222	    screenSwipe?.updateHeldArrowHintMovement?.(event);
   223	    return false;
   224	  };
   225	
   226	  const clearDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
   227	    if (screenSwipe?.clearDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   228	      screenSwipe.clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   229	      return;
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   233	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   234	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   235	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   236	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   237	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
   238	  };
   239	
   240	  const startDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
   241	    if (screenSwipe?.startDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   242	      screenSwipe.startDirectionalArrowLinger(direction);
   243	      return;
   244	    }
   245	
   246	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   247	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   248	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   249	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
   250	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
   251	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   252	    );
   253	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
   254	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   255	    );
   256	  };
   257	
   258	  const updateSwipePreview = (dragDistance) => {
   259	    if (!screenSwipe) return;
   260	
   261	    screenSwipe.updateSwipe({
   262	      x: dragDistance,
   263	    });
   264	  };
   265	
   266	  const animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes = (fromIndex, toIndex) => {
   267	    if (fromIndex === toIndex) {
   268	      if (!indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   269	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   270	      }
   271	      return;
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    if (indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   275	      const previousAnimation = indicatorAnimationRef.current;
   276	      indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   277	      previousAnimation.stop();
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   281	
   282	    const createIndicatorTiming = (toValue, duration = indicatorSlideDuration) =>
   283	      Animated.timing(indicatorProgress, {
   284	        toValue,
   285	        duration,
   286	        useNativeDriver: true,
   287	      });
   288	
   289	    const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   290	    const animation =
   291	      fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0
   292	        ? Animated.sequence([
   293	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length),
   294	            createIndicatorTiming(-1, 0),
   295	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   296	          ])
   297	        : fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex
   298	        ? Animated.sequence([
   299	            createIndicatorTiming(-1),
   300	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length, 0),
   301	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   302	          ])
   303	        : createIndicatorTiming(toIndex);
   304	
   305	    indicatorAnimationRef.current = animation;
   306	
   307	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   308	      if (finished) {
   309	        indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   310	      }
   311	
   312	      if (indicatorAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   313	        indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   314	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   315	      }
   316	    });
   317	  };
   318	
   319	  useEffect(() => {
   320	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(indicatorIndexRef.current, activePageIndex);
   321	  }, [activePageIndex, indicatorProgress]);
   322	
   323	  const animateIndicatorToPage = (pageName) => {
   324	    const pageIndex = navPages.indexOf(pageName);
   325	    if (pageIndex < 0) return;
   326	
   327	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(activeIndexRef.current, pageIndex);
   328	  };
   329	
   330	  const goToPage = (pageName, animateIndicator = true) => {
   331	    hideHeldArrows();
   332	
   333	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) {
   334	      if (pageName === "shop") {
   335	        goToPage("home", animateIndicator);
   336	      }
   337	
   338	      return;
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
   342	    if (!route) return;
   343	
   344	    if (navPages.includes(pageName)) {
   345	      if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   346	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   347	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   348	        return;
   349	      }
   350	
   351	      backgroundHeroState?.freezeHero(safeScrollY);
   352	      if (animateIndicator) animateIndicatorToPage(pageName);
   353	      router.replace(route);
   354	      return;
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    router.push(route);
   358	  };
   359	
   360	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
   361	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   362	
   363	    const previousIndex =
   364	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   365	
   366	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex], true, -1);
   367	  };
   368	
   369	  const goToNextPage = () => {
   370	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   371	
   372	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   373	
   374	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex], true, 1);
   375	  };
   376	
   377	  const shouldClaimCarouselSwipe = (_, gestureState) => {
   378	    if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return false;
   379	
   380	    const horizontalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dx);
   381	    const verticalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dy);
   382	
   383	    if (
   384	      horizontalDistance >= tapHoldHorizontalCancelDistance &&
   385	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * tapHoldHorizontalDominanceRatio
   386	    ) {
   387	      cancelHeldArrows();
   388	    }
   389	
   390	    const shouldClaim =
   391	      horizontalDistance > carouselSwipeActivationDistance &&
   392	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * carouselSwipeActivationRatio;
   393	
   394	    if (shouldClaim) {
   395	      cancelHeldArrows();
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    return shouldClaim;
   399	  };
   400	
   401	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
   402	    PanResponder.create({
   403	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   404	
   405	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   406	
   407	      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: (_, gestureState) =>
   408	        !shouldClaimCarouselSwipe(null, gestureState),
   409	
   410	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   411	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return;
   412	
   413	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   414	        updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   415	
   416	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   417	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   418	          return;
   419	        }
   420	
   421	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   422	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   423	          return;
   424	        }
   425	
   426	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   427	      },
   428	
   429	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   430	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   431	        hideHeldArrows();
   432	
   433	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   434	          clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   435	          screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   436	          return;
   437	        }
   438	
   439	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   440	          const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   441	          const nextPage = navPages[nextIndex];
   442	
   443	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   444	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   445	            x: gestureState.dx,
   446	            page: nextPage,
   447	            direction: 1,
   448	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   449	          });
   450	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   451	          goToPage(nextPage, true, 1);
   452	
   453	          return;
   454	        }
   455	
   456	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   457	          const previousIndex =
   458	            (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   459	          const previousPage = navPages[previousIndex];
   460	
   461	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   462	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   463	            x: gestureState.dx,
   464	            page: previousPage,
   465	            direction: -1,
   466	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   467	          });
   468	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   469	          goToPage(previousPage, true, -1);
   470	
   471	          return;
   472	        }
   473	
   474	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   475	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   476	      },
   477	
   478	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   479	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   480	        hideHeldArrows();
   481	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   482	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   483	      },
   484	
   485	      onShouldBlockNativeResponder: () => true,
   486	    })
   487	  ).current;
   488	
   489	  useEffect(() => {
   490	    hideHeldArrows();
   491	
   492	    return () => {
   493	      hideHeldArrows();
   494	    };
   495	  }, [resolvedActivePage, showCarousel, showOnlyHero]);
   496	
   497	  useEffect(
   498	    () => () => {
   499	      clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   500	    },
   501	    []
   502	  );
   503	
   504	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   505	  const arrowLinkTranslateX = 0;
   506	
   507	  const scrollBeganArrowVisibility = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   508	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   509	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   510	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   511	  });
   512	  const oldHeaderArrowVisibility = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   513	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   514	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   515	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   516	  });
   517	  const oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   518	    heldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   519	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   520	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   521	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   522	    }),
   523	    oldHeaderArrowVisibility
   524	  );
   525	  const scrolledStateArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   526	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility,
   527	    arrowHintPeakOpacity
   528	  );
   529	  const scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   530	    heldArrowOpacity,
   531	    scrolledStateArrowOpacity
   532	  );
   533	  const gatedScrolledArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   534	    scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   535	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   536	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   537	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   538	    }),
   539	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility
   540	  );
   541	  const gatedVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   542	    oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity,
   543	    gatedScrolledArrowOpacity
   544	  ).interpolate({
   545	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   546	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   547	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   548	  });
   549	  const directionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   550	    gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   551	    directionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
   552	      inputRange: [0, 1],
   553	      outputRange: [1, 0],
   554	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   555	    })
   556	  );
   557	  const leftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   558	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   559	    directionalLeftArrowOpacity
   560	  ).interpolate({
   561	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   562	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   563	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   564	  });
   565	  const rightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   566	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   567	    directionalRightArrowOpacity
   568	  ).interpolate({
   569	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   570	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   571	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   572	  });
   573	
   574	  const heroVerticalFadeTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   575	    inputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance],
   576	    outputRange: [heroVerticalFadeHeight, -heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight],
   577	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   578	  });
   579	
   580	  const stickyOffset =
   581	    Platform.OS === "web"
   582	      ? 0
   583	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   584	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   585	          outputRange: [0, carouselStickyOffsetY],
   586	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   587	        });
   588	  const carouselExpansionOffsetY =
   589	    Platform.OS === "ios"
   590	      ? headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   591	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   592	          outputRange: [0, carouselExpansionHeight],
   593	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   594	        })
   595	      : stickyOffset;
   596	  const carouselInnerTranslateY = carouselExpansionOffsetY;
   597	
   598	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   599	    Platform.OS === "web"
   600	      ? 1
   601	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   602	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   603	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   604	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   605	        });
   606	
   607	  const stickyExpansionTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   608	    inputRange: [96, 120],
   609	    outputRange: [-stickyExpansionMaxHeight, 0],
   610	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   611	  });
   612	
   613	  const heroScale = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   614	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   615	    outputRange: [heroStartScale, heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity],
   616	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   617	  });
   618	
   619	  const heroTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   620	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   621	    outputRange: [heroStartTranslateY, heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity],
   622	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   623	  });
   624	
   625	  const headerTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   626	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   627	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   628	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   629	  });
   630	
   631	  const indicatorSegmentWidth = windowWidth / navPages.length;
   632	  const indicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   633	    indicatorProgress,
   634	    indicatorSegmentWidth
   635	  );
   636	  const isMainHeroPage = navPages.includes(resolvedActivePage);
   637	  const heroImageSource = isMainHeroPage
   638	    ? noCheeseboardHeroImage
   639	    : defaultHeroImage;
   640	  const mainHeroBackgroundFadeOpacity = isMainHeroPage
   641	    ? heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   642	        inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   643	        outputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeOverlayOpacity],
   644	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   645	      })
   646	    : 1;
   647	  const heroWidthFitScale = windowWidth / heroSourceWidth;
   648	  const heroWidthFitImageHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroWidthFitScale;
   649	  const heroImageFrameStyle = isMainHeroPage
   650	    ? {
   651	        width: windowWidth,
   652	        height: heroWidthFitImageHeight,
   653	      }
   654	    : null;
   655	  const heroImageTransformStyle = isMainHeroPage
   656	    ? null
   657	    : {
   658	        transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   659	      };
   660	  const heroCoverScale = Math.max(
   661	    windowWidth / heroSourceWidth,
   662	    heroVerticalFadeHeight / heroSourceHeight
   663	  );
   664	  const heroLayoutScale = isMainHeroPage ? heroWidthFitScale : heroCoverScale;
   665	  const heroRenderedWidth = heroSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   666	  const heroRenderedHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   667	  const heroRenderedLeft = (windowWidth - heroRenderedWidth) / 2;
   668	  const heroRenderedTop = isMainHeroPage
   669	    ? 0
   670	    : (heroVerticalFadeHeight - heroRenderedHeight) / 2;
   671	  const noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY = Math.min(
   672	    heroVerticalFadeHeight,
   673	    heroRenderedTop + noCheeseboardVisibleBottomSourceY * heroLayoutScale
   674	  );
   675	  const cheeseboardWidth = cheeseboardSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   676	  const cheeseboardHeight = cheeseboardSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   677	  const cheeseboardLeft =
   678	    heroRenderedLeft + cheeseboardSourceX * heroLayoutScale;
   679	  const cheeseboardTop = isMainHeroPage
   680	    ? noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY - cheeseboardHeight / 2
   681	    : heroRenderedTop + cheeseboardSourceY * heroLayoutScale;
   682	  const routeTransitionState = screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState;
   683	  const isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled =
   684	    isMainHeroPage && isHeaderNewState(heroStateScrollY);
   685	  const isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive =
   686	    isMainHeroPage &&
   687	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   688	    Boolean(routeTransitionState?.isActive);
   689	  const isCheeseboardSwipeActive =
   690	    isMainHeroPage &&
   691	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   692	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive);
   693	  const cheeseboardTransitionDirection =
   694	    routeTransitionState?.direction === -1 ? -1 : 1;
   695	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartX =
   696	    typeof routeTransitionState?.startX === "number"
   697	      ? routeTransitionState.startX
   698	      : 0;
   699	  const cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX =
   700	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.01 : 0;
   701	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX =
   702	    Math.abs(cheeseboardTransitionStartX) > 0.01
   703	      ? cheeseboardTransitionStartX
   704	      : cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX;
   705	  const cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity =
   706	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.001 : 0;
   707	  const cheeseboardDragTranslateX = isCheeseboardSwipeActive
   708	    ? screenSwipe?.swipeX || 0
   709	    : 0;
   710	  const cheeseboardBaseTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   711	    ? 0
   712	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   713	  const cheeseboardBaseOpacity = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   714	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   715	        inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   716	        outputRange: [
   717	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   718	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   719	          1,
   720	        ],
   721	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   722	      })
   723	    : 1;
   724	  const cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   725	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   726	        inputRange: [0, 1],
   727	        outputRange: [
   728	          cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX,
   729	          -cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   730	        ],
   731	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   732	      })
   733	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   734	  const cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   735	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   736	    outputRange: [
   737	      cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX +
   738	        cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   739	      cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX,
   740	    ],
   741	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   742	  });
   743	  const cheeseboardIncomingOpacity = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   744	    inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   745	    outputRange: [1, 1, 0],
   746	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   747	  });
   748	  const cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   749	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, -windowWidth)
   750	    : -windowWidth;
   751	  const cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   752	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, windowWidth)
   753	    : windowWidth;
   754	  const shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews =
   755	    isMainHeroPage &&
   756	    isCheeseboardSwipeActive &&
   757	    !isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive;
   758	  const handleCarouselTouchStart = (event) => {
   759	    showHeldArrows(event);
   760	    return false;
   761	  };
   762	
   763	  const sharedHeaderTouchHandlers = {
   764	    onStartShouldSetResponderCapture: handleCarouselTouchStart,
   765	    onTouchStart: showHeldArrows,
   766	    onTouchMove: updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove,
   767	    onTouchEnd: hideHeldArrows,
   768	    onTouchCancel: hideHeldArrows,
   769	  };
   770	
   771	  const renderArrowChevron = (direction) => (
   772	    <View
   773	      style={[
   774	        styles.arrowChevron,
   775	        direction === "left"
   776	          ? styles.arrowChevronLeft
   777	          : styles.arrowChevronRight,
   778	      ]}
   779	    />
   780	  );
   781	
   782	  const renderCheeseboardLayer = (key, translateX, opacity = 1) => {
   783	    const slideTransformStyle =
   784	      translateX === 0 ? null : { transform: [{ translateX }] };
   785	    const slideOpacityStyle = opacity === 1 ? null : { opacity };
   786	
   787	    return (
   788	      <Animated.View
   789	        key={key}
   790	        collapsable={false}
   791	        pointerEvents="none"
   792	        renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   793	        style={[
   794	          styles.cheeseboardSlideLayer,
   795	          slideTransformStyle,
   796	          slideOpacityStyle,
   797	        ]}
   798	      >
   799	        <Animated.View
   800	          collapsable={false}
   801	          style={styles.cheeseboardHeroTransformLayer}
   802	        >
   803	          <Animated.View
   804	            collapsable={false}
   805	            renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   806	            style={[
   807	              styles.cheeseboardProductsFrame,
   808	              {
   809	                left: cheeseboardLeft,
   810	                top: cheeseboardTop,
   811	                width: cheeseboardWidth,
   812	                height: cheeseboardHeight,
   813	                transform: [{ scale: heroScale }],
   814	              },
   815	            ]}
   816	          >
   817	            <Image
   818	              fadeDuration={0}
   819	              source={cheeseboardProductsImage}
   820	              style={styles.cheeseboardProductsImage}
   821	              resizeMode="contain"
   822	            />
   823	          </Animated.View>
   824	        </Animated.View>
   825	      </Animated.View>
   826	    );
   827	  };
   828	
   829	  const carousel = (
   830	    <View
   831	      style={styles.carouselShell}
   832	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   833	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   834	    >
   835	      <Animated.View style={styles.carouselNavBar}>
   836	        <View style={styles.carouselStickyExpansion}>
   837	          <Animated.View
   838	            pointerEvents="none"
   839	            style={[
   840	              styles.carouselStickyExpansionFill,
   841	              {
   842	                transform: [{ translateY: stickyExpansionTranslateY }],
   843	              },
   844	            ]}
   845	          />
   846	        </View>
   847	
   848	        <Animated.View
   849	          style={[
   850	            styles.carouselInner,
   851	            {
   852	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselInnerTranslateY }],
   853	            },
   854	          ]}
   855	        >
   856	          <Pressable
   857	            onPress={goToPreviousPage}
   858	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   859	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   860	          >
   861	            <Animated.View
   862	              style={[
   863	                styles.arrowBox,
   864	                {
   865	                  opacity: leftArrowOpacity,
   866	                  transform: [
   867	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   868	                  ],
   869	                },
   870	              ]}
   871	            >
   872	              {renderArrowChevron("left")}
   873	            </Animated.View>
   874	          </Pressable>
   875	
   876	          <Pressable
   877	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   878	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   879	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   880	            style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}
   881	          >
   882	            <Text
   883	              numberOfLines={1}
   884	              adjustsFontSizeToFit
   885	              minimumFontScale={0.88}
   886	              style={[
   887	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   888	                styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   889	                {
   890	                  transform: [
   891	                    { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   892	                  ],
   893	                },
   894	              ]}
   895	            >
   896	              {activeLink}
   897	            </Text>
   898	          </Pressable>
   899	
   900	          <Pressable
   901	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   902	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   903	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   904	          >
   905	            <Animated.View
   906	              style={[
   907	                styles.arrowBox,
   908	                {
   909	                  opacity: rightArrowOpacity,
   910	                  transform: [
   911	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   912	                  ],
   913	                },
   914	              ]}
   915	            >
   916	              {renderArrowChevron("right")}
   917	            </Animated.View>
   918	          </Pressable>
   919	        </Animated.View>
   920	
   921	        <Animated.View
   922	          pointerEvents="none"
   923	          style={[
   924	            styles.carouselIndicatorSeparator,
   925	            {
   926	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   927	            },
   928	          ]}
   929	        />
   930	
   931	        <Animated.View
   932	          pointerEvents="none"
   933	          style={[
   934	            styles.carouselIndicatorBar,
   935	            {
   936	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   937	            },
   938	          ]}
   939	        >
   940	          <View style={styles.carouselIndicatorTrack}>
   941	            {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   942	              <View
   943	                key={`carousel-indicator-track-${pageName}`}
   944	                style={styles.carouselIndicatorSegment}
   945	              />
   946	            ))}
   947	          </View>
   948	
   949	          <Animated.View
   950	            style={[
   951	              styles.carouselIndicatorActiveSegment,
   952	              {
   953	                width: indicatorSegmentWidth,
   954	                transform: [{ translateX: indicatorTranslateX }],
   955	              },
   956	            ]}
   957	          />
   958	        </Animated.View>
   959	      </Animated.View>
   960	
   961	    </View>
   962	  );
   963	
   964	  const centerShadow = (
   965	    <Animated.View
   966	      pointerEvents="none"
   967	      style={[
   968	        styles.headerCenterShadow,
   969	        {
   970	          opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
   971	          transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   972	        },
   973	      ]}
   974	    >
   975	      {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
   976	        <>
   977	          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
   978	            <View
   979	              key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
   980	              style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
   981	            />
   982	          ))}
   983	        </>
   984	      )}
   985	    </Animated.View>
   986	  );
   987	
   988	  const hero = (
   989	    <View
   990	      style={styles.hero}
   991	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   992	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   993	    >
   994	      <Animated.Image
   995	        source={heroImageSource}
   996	        style={[
   997	          isMainHeroPage ? styles.heroImageWidthFit : styles.heroImage,
   998	          heroImageFrameStyle,
   999	          heroImageTransformStyle,
  1000	        ]}
  1001	        resizeMode={isMainHeroPage ? "contain" : "cover"}
  1002	      />
  1003	      {isMainHeroPage
  1004	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1005	            "cheeseboard-current",
  1006	            cheeseboardBaseTranslateX,
  1007	            cheeseboardBaseOpacity
  1008	          )
  1009	        : null}
  1010	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1011	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1012	            "cheeseboard-outgoing",
  1013	            cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX
  1014	          )
  1015	        : null}
  1016	      {shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews ? (
  1017	        <>
  1018	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1019	            "cheeseboard-previous-preview",
  1020	            cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX
  1021	          )}
  1022	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1023	            "cheeseboard-next-preview",
  1024	            cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX
  1025	          )}
  1026	        </>
  1027	      ) : null}
  1028	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1029	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1030	            "cheeseboard-incoming",
  1031	            cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX,
  1032	            cheeseboardIncomingOpacity
  1033	          )
  1034	        : null}
  1035	      {isMainHeroPage ? (
  1036	        <Animated.View
  1037	          pointerEvents="none"
  1038	          style={[
  1039	            styles.mainHeroBackgroundFade,
  1040	            { opacity: mainHeroBackgroundFadeOpacity },
  1041	          ]}
  1042	        />
  1043	      ) : null}
  1044	      {!isMainHeroPage ? (
  1045	        <Animated.View
  1046	          pointerEvents="none"
  1047	          style={[
  1048	            styles.heroVerticalFadePanel,
  1049	            {
  1050	              transform: [{ translateY: heroVerticalFadeTranslateY }],
  1051	            },
  1052	          ]}
  1053	        >
  1054	          <LinearGradient
  1055	            colors={["rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)", heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor]}
  1056	            locations={[0, 1]}
  1057	            style={styles.heroVerticalFadeFeather}
  1058	          />
  1059	          <View
  1060	            style={[
  1061	              styles.heroVerticalFadeSolid,
  1062	              { backgroundColor: heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor },
  1063	            ]}
  1064	          />
  1065	        </Animated.View>
  1066	      ) : null}
  1067	    </View>
  1068	  );
  1069	
  1070	  const heroOnlyLayer = (
  1071	    <Animated.View
  1072	      style={[
  1073	        styles.header,
  1074	        {
  1075	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1076	        },
  1077	      ]}
  1078	    >
  1079	      {centerShadow}
  1080	      <View style={styles.heroOnlySpacer} />
  1081	      {Platform.OS === "ios" ? (
  1082	        <Animated.View
  1083	          style={{
  1084	            transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
  1085	          }}
  1086	        >
  1087	          {hero}
  1088	        </Animated.View>
  1089	      ) : (
  1090	        hero
  1091	      )}
  1092	    </Animated.View>
  1093	  );
  1094	
  1095	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
  1096	  if (showOnlyHero) return heroOnlyLayer;
  1097	
  1098	  return (
  1099	    <Animated.View
  1100	      style={[
  1101	        styles.header,
  1102	        {
  1103	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1104	        },
  1105	      ]}
  1106	    >
  1107	      <Animated.View style={styles.orangeBar}>
  1108	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
  1109	          <Text
  1110	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
  1111	            minimumFontScale={0.78}
  1112	            numberOfLines={1}
  1113	            style={styles.logoText}
  1114	          >
  1115	            Alla Vostra
  1116	          </Text>
  1117	        </Pressable>
  1118	
  1119	        {dimHeaderExceptShopButton ? (
  1120	          <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarDimOverlay} />
  1121	        ) : null}
  1122	
  1123	        <Pressable
  1124	          style={[
  1125	            styles.shopButtonWrap,
  1126	            dimHeaderExceptShopButton && styles.shopButtonWrapSpotlight,
  1127	          ]}
  1128	          onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}
  1129	        >
  1130	          <ButtonShadowPlate style={styles.shopButtonShadowPlate} />
  1131	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
  1132	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
  1133	          </View>
  1134	        </Pressable>
  1135	
  1136	        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarBottomHairline} />
  1137	      </Animated.View>
  1138	
  1139	      {showCarousel && showHero && centerShadow}
  1140	      {showCarousel && carousel}
  1141	      {showHero && hero}
  1142	    </Animated.View>
  1143	  );
  1144	}


============================================================
FILE: mob/components/PageDivider.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     4	
     5	const topDividerFadeScrollDistance = 240;
     6	
     7	export default function PageDivider({
     8	  expandedSpacing = false,
     9	  fadeScrollDistance = topDividerFadeScrollDistance,
    10	  fadeWithScrollY = null,
    11	}) {
    12	  const fadeOpacity = fadeWithScrollY?.interpolate({
    13	    inputRange: [0, fadeScrollDistance],
    14	    outputRange: [1, 0],
    15	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  return (
    19	    <Animated.View
    20	      style={[
    21	        sharedStyles.pageDivider,
    22	        expandedSpacing && sharedStyles.expandedPageDivider,
    23	        fadeOpacity && { opacity: fadeOpacity },
    24	      ]}
    25	    />
    26	  );
    27	}

     1	import { Image, Pressable, Text, View } from "react-native";

    15	        resizeMode="cover"

    24	
    25	        <Text style={styles.productIncludesTitle}>Includes</Text>

    29	            {item}
    30	          </Text>
    31	        ))}
    32	      </View>
    33	    </Pressable>
    34	  );
    35	}

FILE: mob/components/ScreenFade.js

     1	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
     2	import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	export default function ScreenFade({ showTopFade = true, topOffset = 84 }) {
     5	  return (
     6	    <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.overlay}>
     7	      {showTopFade ? (
     8	        <LinearGradient
     9	          pointerEvents="none"
    10	          colors={[
    11	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    12	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    13	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    14	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    15	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    16	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    17	          ]}
    18	          locations={[0, 0.14, 0.32, 0.52, 0.72, 1]}
    19	          style={[styles.topFade, { top: topOffset }]}
    20	        />
    21	      ) : null}
    22	
    23	      <LinearGradient
    24	        pointerEvents="none"
    25	        colors={[
    26	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    27	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    28	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    29	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    30	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    31	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    32	        ]}
    33	        locations={[0, 0.28, 0.48, 0.68, 0.86, 1]}
    34	        style={styles.bottomFade}
    35	      />
    36	    </View>
    37	  );
    38	}
    39	
    40	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    41	  overlay: {
    42	    position: "absolute",
    43	    top: 0,
    44	    left: 0,
    45	    right: 0,
    46	    bottom: 0,
    47	    zIndex: 500000,
    48	    elevation: 500000,
    49	  },
    50	
    51	  topFade: {
    52	    position: "absolute",
    53	    left: 0,
    54	    right: 0,
    55	    height: 260,
    56	  },
    57	
    58	  bottomFade: {
    59	    position: "absolute",
    60	    left: 0,
    61	    right: 0,
    62	    bottom: 0,
    63	    height: 150,
    64	  },
    65	});

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}
